		PharmaceuticalDoseTime
		/sub-Mouse25/ses-Mouse25-140203/ecephys/sub-Mouse25_ses-Mouse25-140203_ecephys.nwb - Field description: Time of administration of pharmaceutical dose, relative to time zero.

interpretation of `"PharmaceuticalDoseTime"`.
